# BT22 audit

## Status

2026-09-13: all 102 cards were independently re-audited by three Luna lanes against the committed catalog, local Q&A, direct compiled IR and the actual behavioral tests. The coordinator reviewed and exercised their evidence. All four card categories are accepted at 2/2: **102/102 at 10/10**, 1020/1020. All focused, mechanism, collection, typecheck, synchronization, style and delivery gates passed. No implementation or behavioral-proof gap remains identified. Atomic engine, test and evidence commits were pushed and [review PR #4766](https://github.com/vinicius3333/aegis-digimon-tcg/pull/4766) is open against main. The dated current sections below supersede every historical score and pending note retained for provenance.

All 102 production modules register exclusively with `registerIrCard`. The worktree already contained zero `@ts-nocheck`; the closing sweep confirms zero, rather than claiming nonexistent removals. No production BT22 module or catalog record was changed. Missing distinct proofs were added for BT22-020, 043, 059, 060, 062, 064, 067 and 076. Existing equivalent mechanisms and real card consumers were reused where sufficient; redundant per-card tests were not added.

The 2026-09-12 face-down Meramon checkpoint is closed by the existing public Giromon placement and hidden-source scaling proofs rerun in this collection/mechanism gate. See [stack-card-information.md](engine/stack-card-information.md). Historical audit files mentioned below are provenance in git history, not additional live ledgers.

### Shared behavioral evidence accepted in each applicable current row

Colocated named tests below supply the actual printed-card consumers, legal routes, costs and physical stack/zone assertions. Detailed contract/IR/peer explanations are retained under each card. Generic lifecycle obligations are paired with executable mechanism evidence, not inferred from flags: `q3528OncePerTurnIdentity.test.ts` retains activation identity through stack rotation and resets next own turn; `opponentTurnFrequency.test.ts` rejects a second opponent-turn activation and re-arms next turn; `conformance/ch16b-digivolve-and-battle-keywords.test.ts` distinguishes the inherited `$source` trigger from plays and resets its frequency through the public turn loop. `effects/subtriggers.test.ts`, `effects/stack.test.ts` and the conformance keyword/capability tests exercise optional refusal, unused activation credit after refusal, and attack/redirect boundaries.

Card-specific unique lifecycle paths are explicit in 020, 043, 060, 062 and 076. Q4939 Meat plus ShinMonzaemon reduction uses the existing real `EX9/EX9-070.test.ts` consumer (BT22-038 → BT22-076: printed 5 − Meat 2 − intrinsic 2 = 1; duplicate physical copies do not multiply reductions). Deliberate internal seams (App Fusion execution where no equivalent intent exists, hidden-source setup, direct opponent-effect helper) are identified in their detailed evidence and paired with mechanism tests; they are not called public actions.

### BT22-001 — Puyoyomon

#### Current re-audit — 2026-09-13

**10/10 — complete.** Catalog/rules 2/2; IR trace 2/2; behavioral proof 2/2; peer/stack proof 2/2; delivery gates 2/2. Catalog and exact compiled module were compared again; KB query: no card-specific entry in committed `data/kb/qa.json`. The colocated suite `apps/api/src/cards/BT22/BT22-001.test.ts` passes in the closing regression, with the applicable executable lifecycle/peer mechanisms above. Detailed printed contract and stack boundaries below were reinspected; historical scores are not the basis of this recalculation.

Named reproducible evidence in that suite (parameterized cases expand in Vitest):

- `hatches publicly and legally evolves into Sangomon while rejecting Agumon`.
- `requires effect provenance for its inherited stack-add watcher`.
- `mechanism boundary: Aqua trait text still requires a Digimon kind`.
- `draws once when an effect adds a Sea Animal Digimon to its inherited host`.
- `draws through Shellmon's real On Play placement effect for Sea Animal`.
- `draws through the same natural placement for an Aquabeast trait`.
- `does not draw for a nonmatching Digimon, another stack, or the opponent's turn`.
- `does not draw for a manual placement even when the added card matches`.

#### Contract and sources

The committed catalog defines a blue level 2 Digi-Egg with inherited text: `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When effects place Digimon cards with [Aqua] or [Sea Animal] in any of their traits in this Digimon's digivolution cards, Draw 1.` The local card query has no BT22-001 ruling entry. The direct IR uses `traitContains` for the printed Aqua-in-any-trait wording, including authoritative Aquabeast/Aquatic variants.

#### Implementation and proof

`BT22-001.ts` registers only `registerIrCard`. The inherited watcher is Your Turn, once per turn, self scoped, mine-controlled, effect-provenance scoped, Digimon-only, and filters Aqua/Sea Animal with `traitContains`. Public Shellmon play places real BT1-033 Sea Animal and BT10-023 Aquabeast hand instances under a legal blue level 3 host carrying Puyoyomon; each asserts the moved instance and incremental Draw 1. Other tests prove nonmatching Digimon, another host, opponent turn, manual matching-trait placement, and once-per-turn refusal.

The public stack test follows the working hatch route: it hatches BT22-001 from the egg deck, publicly evolves it into blue level 3 BT22-018 Sangomon, asserts the preserved BT22-001 source, and rejects an invalid BT22-008 Agumon route. The deck is nonempty throughout.

#### Stack and boundaries

BT22-018 Sangomon is the legal blue level 3 host for the inherited Digi-Egg. BT1-033 is the Sea Animal positive; BT10-023 is the Aquabeast substring positive. The implementation’s Digimon kind boundary is structurally asserted; no committed Aqua Tamer record exists, so a non-Digimon Aqua boundary remains untested.

#### Historical verification

`git diff --check` and explicit Oxfmt pass. Generated effects synchronization remains lead-owned and pending.

Provisional scores: Catalog/rules 2/2; IR trace 2/2 pending generated sync; behavioral proof 2/2; peer/stack 2/2; delivery 0/2 pending collection gates.

### BT22-002 — Kyaromon

#### Current re-audit — 2026-09-13

**10/10 — complete.** Catalog/rules 2/2; IR trace 2/2; behavioral proof 2/2; peer/stack proof 2/2; delivery gates 2/2. Catalog and exact compiled module were compared again; KB query: no card-specific entry in committed `data/kb/qa.json`. The colocated suite `apps/api/src/cards/BT22/BT22-002.test.ts` passes in the closing regression, with the applicable executable lifecycle/peer mechanisms above. Detailed printed contract and stack boundaries below were reinspected; historical scores are not the basis of this recalculation.

Named reproducible evidence in that suite (parameterized cases expand in Vitest):

- `hatches publicly and legally evolves into a Puppet while rejecting Agumon`.
- `draws from a Token created by a public Cendrillmon play`.
- `draws only once when another owned Puppet Digimon is deleted`.
- `resets the once-per-turn draw after a complete round of public turns`.
- `does not draw for a non-Puppet, an opponent's Puppet, its own host, or on the opponent's turn`.
- `keeps the watcher live for an opponent-turn deletion`.
- `models Tokens and Puppet Digimon as alternatives rather than requiring a Puppet Token`.

#### Contract and sources

The catalog defines a yellow level 2 Digi-Egg with inherited text: `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When any of your Tokens or other [Puppet] trait Digimon are deleted, Draw 1.` The local card query returns no BT22-002 ruling entry.

#### Implementation and proof

`BT22-002.ts` registers only `registerIrCard`. The inherited watcher is Your Turn, once per turn, mine-controlled, excludes itself, and uses an OR of any Token or other Digimon with Puppet trait before Draw 1. The tests prove two qualifying Puppet deletions consume one OPT, non-Puppet/opponent/host exclusions, and an owned Puppet deletion during the opponent turn leaves the watcher unused. A public Cendrillmon play creates a real Familiar Token; deleting that token through the engine produces the observed draw.

#### Stack and evolution

The public stack test hatches BT22-002 from the egg deck, evolves it into yellow Puppet BT22-029 Shoemon, preserves the egg source, and rejects BT22-008 Agumon. The natural Token test uses a Puppet stack carrying BT22-002 and Cendrillmon's public On Play Token creation. No synthetic registration or production engine edits were used.

#### Remaining boundary

The Token and Puppet alternatives are structurally asserted and each has a causal deletion path. The reset test records the initial turn count, runs public seat 0 and seat 1 turns with deck headroom, asserts turn count advanced and seat 0 is active again, then deletes a second Puppet and observes the second draw. A natural public Overclock deletion route is not needed because the Token route directly proves the printed Token alternative.

### BT22-003 — Tapmon

#### Current re-audit — 2026-09-13

**10/10 — complete.** Catalog/rules 2/2; IR trace 2/2; behavioral proof 2/2; peer/stack proof 2/2; delivery gates 2/2. Catalog and exact compiled module were compared again; KB query: no card-specific entry in committed `data/kb/qa.json`. The colocated suite `apps/api/src/cards/BT22/BT22-003.test.ts` passes in the closing regression, with the applicable executable lifecycle/peer mechanisms above. Detailed printed contract and stack boundaries below were reinspected; historical scores are not the basis of this recalculation.

Named reproducible evidence in that suite (parameterized cases expand in Vitest):

- `hatches publicly into Tapmon's legal Appmon evolution stack and rejects Agumon`.
- `reduces one opposing Digimon by 2000 when its inherited host gets linked`.
- `does not trigger for another stack, on the opponent's turn, or twice in one turn`.
- `expires the -2000 DP grant after the complete opponent turn`.

#### Contract and sources

The catalog defines a yellow level 2 Appmon Digi-Egg with inherited text: `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon gets linked, 1 of your opponent's Digimon gets -2000 DP for the turn.` The local card query returns no BT22-003 ruling entry.

#### Implementation and proof

`BT22-003.ts` registers only `registerIrCard`. The inherited watcher is Your Turn, once per turn, self-scoped to `whenLinked`, and modifies exactly one opposing Digimon by -2000 with `forTheTurn` duration. Public `linkCard` tests prove a genuine link causes the exact reduction, another host does not trigger it, a second link in the same turn is suppressed, an opponent-turn link is suppressed, and a second opposing Digimon remains at base DP.

#### Stack, timing, and boundaries

The public stack test hatches BT22-003 and evolves it into Appmon BT21-009 Gatchmon while rejecting BT22-008 Agumon. The duration test observes the grant during the owner's main phase, then verifies base DP at the end of that turn, throughout the complete opponent turn, and at the next owner main phase. The fixtures use real Appmon link cards and exact public link state. The -2000 DP is a triggered, resolved modifier that lasts for the turn independently of the link card, so unlinking does not revoke it.

### BT22-005 — Tsumemon

#### Current re-audit — 2026-09-13

**10/10 — complete.** Catalog/rules 2/2; IR trace 2/2; behavioral proof 2/2; peer/stack proof 2/2; delivery gates 2/2. Catalog and exact compiled module were compared again; KB query: no card-specific entry in committed `data/kb/qa.json`. The colocated suite `apps/api/src/cards/BT22/BT22-005.test.ts` passes in the closing regression, with the applicable executable lifecycle/peer mechanisms above. Detailed printed contract and stack boundaries below were reinspected; historical scores are not the basis of this recalculation.

Named reproducible evidence in that suite (parameterized cases expand in Vitest):

- `hatches publicly and legally evolves Tsumemon into Keramon, while rejecting an invalid Agumon route`.
- `draws for an Unidentified Digimon independently of the CS path`.
- `draws once for either a CS or Unidentified Digimon played on its controller's turn`.
- `resets the once-per-turn draw after a complete round of public turns`.
- `does not draw for a near-matching card, an opponent's card, or on the opponent's turn`.
- `does not draw for an opponent-controlled qualifying subject while this watcher is live`.

#### Contract and sources

The committed catalog record (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`) defines a black level 2 Digi-Egg with inherited text: `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When any of your Digimon with the [Unidentified] or [CS] trait are played, Draw 1.` `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT22-005` returned no Q&A or errata entry. The printed effect has no cost, target choice, refusal, security clause, or duration beyond the turn trigger and once-per-turn limit.

#### IR and seam trace

`apps/api/src/cards/BT22/BT22-005.ts` registers only `registerIrCard("BT22-005", compiled)`. Its inherited `YourTurn` effect contains a `whenPlayed` sub-trigger restricted to the controller's Digimon and an OR trait filter for `Unidentified`/`CS`, followed by one draw and `frequency: "OncePerTurn"`. The interpreter's play event and once-per-turn ledger provide the trigger and limit; draw uses the source controller's deck.

#### Behavioral proof

`BT22-005.test.ts` proves the positive CS and Unidentified paths, then proves the second qualifying play does not draw again in the same turn. A public hatch followed by a legal breeding-area evolution into Keramon proves the egg-to-host route and rejects an invalid Agumon source. The negative tests separately cover a near-matching non-trait and an opponent-controlled qualifying subject through the production `whenPlayed` provenance seam; the natural opponent-turn play covers the turn boundary. - `does not draw for an ordinary card placement, another stack, or the opponent's turn`.
- `confirms the Q5212 after-state is a legal evolution once Moonmon is promoted`.
- `does not activate after Koh & Sayo rotates Moonmon and immediately digivolves the host (Q5212)`.

#### Contract and sources

The catalog defines a purple level 2 Digi-Egg with inherited text: `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When effects place this Digimon's top stacked card as its bottom digivolution card, Draw 1 and trash 1 card in your hand.` `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT22-006` returns Q5212: after an effect places the top card as bottom and the host is then digivolved, this inherited effect cannot activate. No cost, target selection, Security effect, or optional refusal is printed.

#### IR and seam trace

`BT22-006.ts` has only `registerIrCard("BT22-006", compiled)`. The inherited Your Turn once-per-turn effect is an `onAddDigivolutionCards` sub-trigger with `sourceFilter: { byEffect: true }`, self-host trigger identity, bottom-position matching, and `requirePlacedOwnTopAtStackBottom: true`; it then draws one and trashes one card from the controller's hand. This ties the effect to effect provenance, the host's own top card, and the exact bottom rotation payload.

#### Behavioral proof

`BT22-006.test.ts` first checks the compiled shape, then activates a real peer effect on a legal Gargomon/Terriermon/Moonmon stack and settles the result: the deck decreases by one, a hand card is trashed, and the host top card remains the host after rotation. The negative test proves ordinary placement, a different host's rotation payload, and the opponent's turn do not draw or trash. A separate after-state test proves that a promoted Moonmon in breeding can legally evolve into a level 3 Lunamon. The public EX5-064 Q5212 test proves the complete rotation-then-digivolve path with a legal battle-area Lunamon/Moonmon stack and exact hand instance.

#### Catalog and rules

The catalog requires alternate level 4 Greymon-in-name or CS-trait digivolution for 3, static Raid, and a When Digivolving optional free play from hand while the controller has 1 or fewer Tamers: either a red/black Tamer costing 4 or less, or any CS Tamer. The inherited effect is Security Attack +1. The local KB query reports no Q&A entries.

#### Implementation and proof

`BT22-012.ts` registers only compiled IR. The effect uses a permanent-count <=1 Tamer condition, a primary red/black Tamer cost <=4 filter plus a separate CS-trait `orFilters` branch, optional free play, and inherited Security Attack +1. Focused tests `plays a blue CS Tamer for free when digivolving with at most one Tamer`, `accepts a red cost-4-or-less Tamer through the first option`, `does not play a Tamer when two are already present`, and `projects Raid and inherited Security Attack +1 to observable keyword state` cover both alternatives, gate, static keyword, and inherited projection.

Invalid blue/non-CS and high-cost red candidates, optional refusal, legal Greymon-name evolution, invalid level-3 routing, and a real inherited Security Attack +1 attack against two security cards are covered. ### BT22-028 — Ariemon

#### Current re-audit — 2026-09-13

**10/10 — complete.** Catalog/rules 2/2; IR trace 2/2; behavioral proof 2/2; peer/stack proof 2/2; delivery gates 2/2. Catalog and exact compiled module were compared again; KB query: Q4878, Q5213. The colocated suite `apps/api/src/cards/BT22/BT22-028.test.ts` passes in the closing regression, with the applicable executable lifecycle/peer mechanisms above. Detailed printed contract and stack boundaries below were reinspected; historical scores are not the basis of this recalculation.

Named reproducible evidence in that suite (parameterized cases expand in Vitest):

- `plays one qualifying stack card at each level and shares the once-per-turn costed reaction`.
- `plays every available level bucket from a mixed realistic evolution stack as required by Q5213`.
- `pays with another Digimon, bottom-decks exactly one opponent, unsuspends, and shares frequency`.
- `leaves all zones and suspension unchanged when the optional stack cost is declined`.
- `executes Decode from its own stack on a non-battle leave`.

Catalog fields identify a level 7 blue Data Aquatic/LIBERATOR Digimon with alternate [Yao Qinglan], [MarineBullmon] evolution for cost 6. Decode is executable for a level 6 or lower [Aqua] or [Sea Animal] card from its own stack on a non-battle leave. The When Digivolving play effect is optional as a whole, but Q5213 requires one each of levels 3, 4, and 5 whenever available; the IR therefore has three mandatory per-level plays and preserves the Aqua-or-Sea-Animal boundary. The When Digivolving and When Attacking costed effects share one once-per-turn identity, place another Digimon under Ariemon, bottom-deck one opponent Digimon, and unsuspend Ariemon. This preserves the Once Per Turn shown on the official English and Japanese card images despite the English HTML omission.

Behavioral proof in `BT22-028.test.ts` uses a realistic level 3/4/5/6 evolution stack to prove Q5213's one-each requirement and level-6 retention, proves bottom-deck order, placement cost, unsuspend, shared Once Per Turn across the two timings, and proves optional refusal leaves zones and suspension unchanged. Decode is also proven from the own stack on non-battle deletion. The focused suite passed 5 tests.

Q4878 and Q5213 are the applicable local KB rulings. No card-local ambiguity or unsupported clause remains. Collection delivery gates remain owned by the lead audit.

  [When Digivolving] You may play 1 each of level 3, level 4 and level 5 Digimon cards with [Aqua] or [Sea Animal] in any of their traits from this Digimon's digivolution cards without paying the cost.
  [When Digivolving] [When Attacking] [Once Per Turn] By placing 1 of your other Digimon as this Digimon's bottom digivolution card, return 1 of your opponent's Digimon to the bottom of the deck and this Digimon unsuspends.

## Mechanisms

The BT22 re-audit produced no `*-MECHANISM.md` document. The engine seams it touched are described in
the Card ledger entries above and in `docs/audits/BT22-AUDIT.md` (0f59800a5, 2026-09-06), which recorded
two shared corrections: the interpreter now accepts only a printed alternate or base-granted route when a
digivolution base has no level (exposed by BT22-090), and the live conferral collector now feeds the
player-facing `[Main]` activation path as well as triggered timings, preserving host/granter provenance
and the provenance-specific Once Per Turn key (exposed by BT22-078).

## Knowledge base index

BT22 has no `KB-INDEX.md`. Per-card knowledge-base findings are recorded inline in the Card ledger.

## Open items

No unresolved BT22 card or audited engine limitation remains identified. All 102 cards are recalculated at 10/10 with reproducible named evidence and green gates. The 2026-09-13 ledger supersedes all older counts, provisional caps and pending statements retained below for historical provenance. The isolated branch is pushed and [PR #4766](https://github.com/vinicius3333/aegis-digimon-tcg/pull/4766) carries the final implementation and validation scope.

### Historical reconciliation notes

- Contradiction: the banner in `docs/audits/BT22-AUDIT.md` (0f59800a5, 2026-09-06) says "The independent
  audit started on 2026-09-06 is incomplete; current evidence and scores are tracked in
  BT22-reaudit/PLAN.md and BT22-reaudit/ledger.json. Claims below are not carried forward without
  revalidation." `docs/audits/BT22-reaudit/PLAN.md` likewise says "Status: incomplete". Both were
  written at the start of the re-audit; `ledger.json` and `SCORES.md` in the newer commit 7d6172a1a
  (2026-09-07) record `complete` and 1020/1020. The newer ledger wins.
- Contradiction: `BT22-AUDIT.md` reports the full collection at 565 tests across 103 files, while the
  re-audit's final run reports 652 tests across the same 103 files. The re-audit added tests; the higher
  count was current at that historical checkpoint; today's closing count is stated above.
- Note carried from the winning source: the "zero semantic changes outside BT22" claim describes the
  semantic branch diff against `main` (93 changed records, all BT22). It does not assert that persisted
  records for other collections already equal their direct modules.
- Note carried from the winning source: direct production seams remain where the engine has no
  player-facing equivalent — App Fusion execution, face-down source setup, and a direct opponent-effect
  deletion helper. These are paired with focused mechanism tests and are not represented as public
  intents.
- Deleted tooling: `docs/audits/BT22-reaudit/recalculate.mjs` recomputed `SCORES.md` from
  `ledger.json`. Both were removed with this consolidation; the historical scores are retained in the Card ledger
  above; current scores are recalculated separately and the inputs are recoverable at commit eabe99351.

### Catalog review

Historical 2026-09-06 source correction checkpoint. The current closing runtime and card-specific gates are stated above.

The lead compared all 102 unique BT22 entries against the [official English collection page](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?category=522030&search=true) on 2026-09-06. Parsed fields, including separate special evolution conditions and card Q&A, are preserved in `logs/official-catalog-fields.json`. The ledger preserves printed evolution routes separately because the catalog's numeric color/level `evoCosts` cannot express Appmon form requirements.

#### Confirmed corrections

| -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| BT22-009 | Restore Appmon link requirement costing 2.                                                          | [Effecmon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-009&search=true)    |
| BT22-011 | Main play effect accepts Flame or CS, not only Flame.                                               | [BlueMeramon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-011&search=true) |
| BT22-014 | Restore the rule that its name also has Greymon.                                                    | [Gaiomon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-014&search=true)     |
| BT22-015 | Restore CS level 6 evolution costing 5 and the named Greymon/Garurumon level 6 DNA route costing 0. | [Omnimon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-015&search=true)     |
| BT22-016 | Restore Appmon link costing 1 and correct the search trait to Awakening.                            | [Mcmon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-016&search=true)       |
| BT22-030 | Restore Appmon link costing 1.                                                                      | [Musimon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-030&search=true)     |
| BT22-033 | Restore Appmon link costing 2.                                                                      | [Mediamon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-033&search=true)    |
| BT22-035 | Restore Appmon link costing 3.                                                                      | [Entermon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-035&search=true)    |
| BT22-049 | Correct the DM level 3 evolution cost from 3 to 2. The English card image independently confirms 2. | [Vegiemon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-049&search=true)    |
| BT22-075 | Restore Appmon link costing 3.                                                                      | [Fakemon](https://world.digimoncard.com/cards/?card_no=BT22-075&search=true)     |

Form-based evolution routes identified for direct IR verification: BT22-009, 033, 035, 039, 050, 058, and 075. They must be expressed using exact form traits and proven with differently colored legal bases and invalid near peers. Merely preserving the ordinary color/level requirement is insufficient.

#### Source discrepancy resolved without changing behavior

BT22-028 Ariemon's English HTML effect omits its once-per-turn tag. Both the [official English card image](https://world.digimoncard.com/images/cardlist/card/BT22-028.png) and [official Japanese card image](https://digimoncard.com/images/cardlist/card/BT22-028.png) visibly include it. The catalog's once-per-turn restriction is retained. Auditors must not remove it based on the HTML omission.

The Aquatic rule traits on BT22-018/021/024/027 and Dinosaur rule trait on BT22-062 are already flattened into the catalog's `types`. BT22-079's expanded deck limit is already represented by `maxCountInDeck`. Their missing reminder sentences do not alone establish missing runtime behavior; the existing metadata and its behavioral use still need verification.

#### Validation

The shared catalog rebuild passed (`logs/shared-catalog-rebuild-2.log`). Shared card tests and scoped semantic validation are recorded in `logs/catalog-shared-tests.log` and `logs/catalog-scope-check.log`. These checks do not certify the remaining BT22 effects. Set-scoped effects synchronization remains a separate integration gate.
